| CG-12 | low | SP-17 is the only wave-2+ subplan with no commit-count exit criterion, though its count is in band | ten subplans carry a `Commit count verified` checklist row; `grep -nE 'Commit count' plans/V6-SP-17-*.md` → no match; its heading at `:1207` says "Exactly **8 commits**, in order" | `V6-SP-17` commit plan and Done checklist |
| CG-13 | info | Three consequential claims rest on a single agent's reading and could not be executed or independently re-run | see below | SP-08-C7, SP-08-C26, XS-01, TR-05 |

**CG-02 (high) — the yardstick moved in the same merge.** The 15-file set is the eleven wave-2+ subplans plus the four checkpoints. It excludes five plan-set artifacts the same merge changed, and the one that matters is `00-ARCHITECTURE.md`: `ca63567`, "docs(arch): correct the normative spec against shipped code", +127/-18. The per-file verifiers' strongest verdicts are stated as conflicts *with that file* — SP-08-C2 ("§5.4 never mentions Bind"), SP-08-C6 ("§5.8 declares no such method"), SP-08-C3, which rests on §5.9's NodeID constructor table. That constructor table did not exist before this round; `ca63567` added it, labelled "(SP-11 amendment)". So a verifier judged a rewritten plan against normative text written hours earlier in the same merge, and no extractor/verifier pair was assigned to the 127 added lines. The critic spot-checked the §5.9 block and found all ten signatures real — but nothing in the audit's design would have caught it otherwise, and `OWNERS.tsv`, `README.md` and `TRACEABILITY.md` were reviewed only incidentally, by the cross-cutting agents, from the outside.

**CG-04 (high) — two plans instruct code the guard now blocks.** `33bb206` shipped `TestGuard_NoSilentSketchLoadOutsideItsPackage`: outside `internal/sketch`, no non-test `.go` file under `internal/`, `cmd/` or `test/` may call `sketch.Load`. Two rewritten plans instruct exactly that in production paths. SP-16's O4 warm start step 1 is written as `if sketch.Load(".qompack/sketches/touch.project.cms", proj) fails (missing or CRC/version): proj = bootstrapProjectCMS(ctx)` — daemon-side — and its error-handling matrix asserts "`sketch.Load` returns `ErrNotFound` + Loud (SP-03 behaviour)", which is the precise misclassification the A3 fix addressed. SP-17's `qompack fsck` check 9 reads "`sketch.Load` on `sketches/tried.bloom` succeeds (CRC + version)", in `internal/cli`. An implementer following either plan writes code the guard fails. Neither plan could have known — the audit predates the code round — and CG-11 shows the round did not sweep for it.

**CG-03 (high)** duplicates CD-1 and CD-3 from the process side and needs no separate treatment; it is listed because it was found independently, which is corroboration rather than double-counting. The same applies to CG-05/WP-01, CG-06/WP-10, CG-07/WP-08 and CG-10/WP-02.

**What remains unverified.** Three consequential claims in this report rest on a single agent's reading, and are labelled as such rather than asserted:

- **XS-01's arithmetic.** The shipped `SkiRentalShouldWrite` guards only `r <= 0` (`internal/scheduler/formulas.go:32-37`, which I confirmed), and SP-16's own test table has no `w = 0` row (confirmed at `:1539-1550`). But `SkiRentalThreshold` exists only as plan text, so the claim that `SkiRentalThreshold(0.1, 0)` returns 0 and therefore reinstates the regression is a reading of two plan documents against each other and **cannot be executed**. The blocker does not depend on it — the contradictory starting states and the doubly-claimed file stand on their own — but the behavioural half is inference.
- **TR-05's negative claim** that SP-05, SP-07 and SP-14 "never self-identify a layer" anywhere in their bodies is a whole-document negative that was not independently re-run.
- **SP-08-C7's residual**, which the extractor itself flagged as unresolved: `internal/store/put.go`'s doc says a nil `Strip` leaves "only the CRLF normalization", which contradicts `canon`'s `gateSet`, and SP-08's pre-step does not mention the store-side doc.

Beyond those three, four structural limits apply to this report as a whole. The eight SP-08 verifier-only items in CG-09 are confirmed as *text changes* and were never assessed against the design; they must not be read as adjudicated. `00-ARCHITECTURE.md`, `OWNERS.tsv` and `README.md` were never given an extractor/verifier pair (CG-02), so findings that cite them as authority inherit that gap. Every `-run` pattern in the fifteen rewritten documents is outside the repo's own lint by construction (CG-07/WP-08), so WP-09's five dead patterns were found by hand and there is no reason to believe they are the only five. And the audit ran before the code-fix round merged at `33bb206`, so plan text describing wave-1 behaviour is one round stale wherever the round changed a contract — CD-7 and CG-04 are the two instances found, and CG-11 establishes that no systematic sweep was performed to bound the class.

## Recommended actions before wave 2 starts

Ordered by severity, and split by when the defect actually bites. The first group has to be closed before `feat/sp08-observer-l0` or `feat/sp09-negative-knowledge` is cut. The second group is real but lands in wave 3, 4 or 5; scheduling it now is cheaper than rediscovering it, and several items are one-line edits to documents that are open anyway.

### Blocks wave 2 from starting

**A1. Give `WireObserver` a caller, or give SP-08 permission to be that caller.** `XS-02`, and the design conflict `SP-08-C2` underneath it. SP-08's exit criterion `SP-08-C16` forbids the feature branch from editing `internal/cli/daemon.go`, and `internal/cli/daemon.go:86` is the only non-test `daemon.New` call site in the repository. Either add the one-line `daemon.WireObserver(&opts, reg)` call to Commit 0's checklist on the `arch/sp08-observer-seams` pre-step branch — the same branch that already edits `internal/store/put.go`, `internal/cli/hookclient.go` and `internal/daemon/handlers.go` — or carve `internal/cli/daemon.go` out of the branch-purity criterion and add the call to SP-08's own commit 6. Lands in `plans/V3-SP-08-observer-l0.md`, the Commit 0 checklist and the Definition-of-Done grep criterion. Until one of the two is written down, SP-08 merges green with all five `Services` seams nil and every hook still exiting 0.

**A2. Amend `00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.4 to declare the seam SP-08 wires through, or amend SP-08 to use `Handle`.** `SP-08-C2`, `TR-13`, `XS-02`. Concretely: add `func (*Options) Bind(...)` and the `Services` field set (`ObserveTool`, `ObserveResult`, `ObservePrompt`, `ObserveSessionEnd`, `ObserveSignals`) to §5.4's extension-seam note and its frozen-signature block, on an `arch/sp08-observer-seams` commit, so that "the sanctioned seam" is a claim §5 supports. The alternative — reverting SP-08 to `Options.Handle` — is not viable, because it would overwrite SP-05's five default routes and `ingest` is unexported, which is why the rewrite made the change in the first place. Lands in `plans/00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.4, and in the Commit 0 checklist of `plans/V3-SP-08-observer-l0.md`, which currently amends only the §5.8 note.

**A3. Amend `00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.8 for `ArgsDigest`, and §5.7 for `LoadWithLog` and `ReplaceGenerational`.** `SP-08-C6`, `SP-09-C10`, `SP-09-C21`, `SP-09-C09`. Four edits, all in the same direction — the spec still describes pre-wave-1 code: declare `ArgsDigest` as a package-level function of `internal/store` rather than a `Store` interface method and change §5.8's `ArgsPreview` comment from "≤120 chars" to "≤120 bytes"; change §5.7's `Load` annotation, which currently promises "corrupt → ErrNotFound + Loud log", and add `LoadWithLog` and `ReplaceGenerational`, both of which SP-09 now makes mandatory and neither of which §5.7 mentions; and correct §3.3's `paths.CreateNew(p)` and `paths.WriteAtomic(p, b)` to the shipped three- and two-argument forms. All four belong on one `arch/<reason>` branch off develop, landing before either wave-2 feature branch is cut, per §0 lines 16-26 and §9 line 2192. Lands in `plans/00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §3.3, §5.7, §5.8.

**A4. Make V3-VERIFY able to dispose of the fourteen rows deferred to it.** `CD-1`, `CD-3`, `CG-03`. `grep -c 'deferred:V3-VERIFY' plans/CARRIED-DEFECTS.tsv` returns 14; `plans/V3-VERIFY-observer-and-negative-knowledge.md:62` opens "**Six carried-defect rows**". Add disposition steps for SP02-D1 through SP02-D6, SP04-D7 and SP06-D2, or re-defer them explicitly in `plans/CARRIED-DEFECTS.tsv` to a checkpoint that does handle them. In the same pass, fix §0a item 5, which describes the guard as blocking "on any row left **`open`**" — the pre-A7 semantics; the shipped `unresolved()` is `d.status == "open" || strings.HasPrefix(d.status, deferredPrefix)`, and no row in the manifest is `open`. Lands in `plans/V3-VERIFY-observer-and-negative-knowledge.md` §0a items 5 and 6 and §8.5, and in `plans/CARRIED-DEFECTS.tsv`.

**A5. Point V3-VERIFY's completion report at `plans/V3-report.md`.** `CD-2`. `TestCarriedDefects_WaveReportRequiresResolution` keys on exactly one path — `report := filepath.Join(root, "plans", wave+"-report.md")` — and skips silently when the file is absent (`test/guards/carrieddefects_test.go:282-289`). V3-VERIFY §8.5 directs its completion report to the merge commit body or an ADR instead, so the sign-off gate can never fire. Change §8.5 to name `plans/V3-report.md` as `V1-VERIFY:859` does, and apply the same fix to `plans/V4-VERIFY-checkpoint-rehydrate-schedule-retrieval.md:1410`, which replicates it. Lands in `plans/V3-VERIFY-…md` §8.5 and `plans/V4-VERIFY-…md:1410`.

**A6. Re-sweep the wave-2+ plans against the post-audit code round.** `CD-7`, `CG-04`, `CG-11`. `33bb206` landed after `c0c5627` and touched exactly one line of one wave-2+ plan. Two consequences are already visible. V3-VERIFY §X5 runs the replay driver with `--sketch $TMP/v3-health.json` and no `--baseline`, which re-creates the comparison `cb27449` ("test(integration): judge the bloom ceiling against the ceiling alone") had to disable with `--baseline ""`: `test/replay/main.go:50` sets `defaultBaselinePath = "testdata/baseline/phase0.json"` and `:147` defaults the flag to it, and `test/replay/gate.go:65-68` marks `bloom_fp_rate` and `bloom_fill_ratio` `DirLowerBetter`, so X5 silently judges real ledger health against the baseline's watch-fors at 2%. (The separate cross-tier and missing-baseline refusals the same round added are at `test/replay/main.go:358-370` and `:395-401`, from `0fff22c`, not in `gate.go`.) And `V5-SP-16:1340`, `:1476`, `:504` and `V6-SP-17:778` instruct production code to call `sketch.Load`, which `TestGuard_NoSilentSketchLoadOutsideItsPackage` now blocks outside `internal/sketch`. Lands in `plans/V3-VERIFY-…md` §5 row X5 and §2 row B10, `plans/V5-SP-16-phase7-refinements.md`, `plans/V6-SP-17-packaging-hardening-and-release.md`.

**A7. Settle the wave-2 build-order guard and fix README's stated rationale.** `WP-01`, `WP-03`, `V3-C4`, `V3-C5b`, `TR-04`, `CG-05`, `CG-10`. `plans/README.md:51` requires every wave from 2 onward to add its own build-order guard before its branches merge, written and observed failing against the wrong order first, and requires the wave's checkpoint to name it — or, failing that, to declare the order unverified in the checkpoint report. V3-VERIFY does neither and reports the guard's absence as evidence that no order is constrained. Decide which is the rule, then make one of the two documents say it: either author a wave-2 guard in `test/guards/buildorder_test.go` and name it in V3-VERIFY §0a, or record the "unverified order" declaration in V3-VERIFY §10's completion template. In the same edit, replace README:51's rationale — "SP-09's elimination ledger lands before SP-08's observer, **which imports it** (§3.2)" — which SP-08's own exit criterion forbids and the tree refutes; the *order* is fine, the reason is not. Also reconcile the guard count: README:49 says the file "holds exactly two", `grep -c "^func TestGuard_" test/guards/buildorder_test.go` returns six, V3-VERIFY says six, `V1-SP-01:1639` says four, `V6-VERIFY:87` says four. Lands in `plans/README.md:49` and `:51`, `plans/V3-VERIFY-…md` §0a item 4 and §10, `plans/V1-SP-01-…md:1639`, `plans/V6-VERIFY-…md:87`.

**A8. Reconcile the `SessionStart` delegation seam between SP-08 and SP-11.** `XS-03`. The two plans name the same seam differently — type name, both method names, and one return signature — and `grep -rn SessionStartRehydrator internal/` returns nothing, so neither is anchored to shipped code. SP-08 defines the type and is authored first, so the naming has to be settled before SP-08's branch is cut or SP-11 will be written against a symbol that does not exist. Lands in `plans/V3-SP-08-observer-l0.md:447-450`, `:499`, `:1606`, `:2063` and `plans/V4-SP-11-rehydrator-l5.md:569-577`, `:2058`.

**A9. Adjudicate the eight SP-08 verifier-only items and the three unverified claims.** `CG-09`, `CG-13`, and `SP-08-C7`'s residual. Eight SP-08 records are confirmed as text changes with `designObligation: "?"`, `risk: "?"` and `designConflict: "unchecked"`; the record says three of the eight need a design ruling. Separately, `SP-08-C7` flags that `internal/store/put.go`'s doc comment says a nil `Strip` leaves "only the CRLF normalization", which contradicts `canon`'s `gateSet`, and SP-08's pre-step does not mention the store-side doc — add that bullet to Commit 0. Lands in `plans/V3-SP-08-observer-l0.md` Commit 0 checklist and `internal/store/put.go`'s doc comment.

**A10. Audit `00-ARCHITECTURE.md` itself.** `CG-02`. The normative baseline every verifier in this audit measured against was rewritten in the same merge — `ca63567`, +127/−18 — and is not one of the fifteen files. Neither are `OWNERS.tsv`, `README.md`, `TRACEABILITY.md` or `V2-report.md`. Actions A2 and A3 will edit §3.3, §5.4, §5.7 and §5.8 anyway; extend that pass to a read of `ca63567` in full, since thirteen of the twenty-six design conflicts are against a document nobody checked, and seven of the thirteen resolve against it. Lands in `plans/00-ARCHITECTURE.md`.

### Must be fixed before the wave it affects

**B1. Wave 3 and wave 5 — stop SP-12 and SP-16 authoring the same file with different bodies.** `XS-01`, blocker. SP-12 (wave 3) creates `internal/scheduler/skirental.go`; SP-16 (wave 5) opens by asserting `internal/scheduler/formulas.go` still holds the function and instructs Commit 2 to move it into a `skirental.go` it expects to create — and the two plans' `SkiRentalShouldWrite` bodies disagree on the `w <= 0` case, where the shipped `formulas.go:32-37` guards only `r <= 0`. Of 174 file/subplan spec pairs across the eleven subplans, this is the only doubly-claimed path. Pick one owner for the file, rewrite the other plan's starting-state premise, and reconcile the two bodies. Note `CG-13`: `SkiRentalThreshold` exists only as plan text, so the arithmetic claim rests on a single reading. Lands in `plans/V4-SP-12-scheduler-l3.md:830`, `:1079`, `:1089-1094`, `:2830` and `plans/V5-SP-16-phase7-refinements.md:17`, `:693-711`, `:1539-1550`, `:1719-1720`.

And the `pins` coverage floor is 75 in `plans/OWNERS.tsv:48` — the file `devtool cover` actually reads — while `V4-SP-10:1523`, `V4-VERIFY:405`, `:502`, `V5-VERIFY:975` and `V6-VERIFY:287`, `:942-946` assert 90. Use `V3-VERIFY:1246-1256` and `V4-VERIFY:1211-1216` as the working template, which `WP-07`'s `--phase`-less commands depart from. Lands in `plans/V4-VERIFY-…md`, `plans/V5-VERIFY-…md`, `plans/V6-VERIFY-…md`, `plans/OWNERS.tsv` or `plans/V4-SP-10-checkpointer-l4.md:1523`.

For `SP-11-C12`, either amend `00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.15:1603 and open a PR against `Qompack.md` §8.6:971 for the ancestor walk, or narrow the plan back to the containing-directory reading and fix `internal/rules/rules.go:32-33`'s doc comment instead. For `SP-11-C17`, either amend `Qompack.md` §8.6:959-960 to permit dropping tier-1 material under an impossible budget, or change the wave-0 conformance case (`behaviour.go:163-168`, `:240-247`) that makes the old wording unimplementable. For `SP-11-C24`, either amend `Qompack.md` §10 Phase 3's "against Phase 0 baseline" or restore A2's comparison against the committed artifact. `SP-11-C28` needs only a handoff line: record in `plans/V4-VERIFY-…md` that `frontierOf` is replaced by the real `Ref.Frontier` at the V4 run. **B4. Wave 3 — delete SP-12's fabricated file-length rule.** `SP-12-C6`. `plans/V4-SP-12-scheduler-l3.md:2406` and `:2884` require `internal/cli/daemon.go` to stay under 150 LOC and attribute the bound to `00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §3.1; §3.1 line 306 states that bound only for `cmd/qompack/main.go`. Either drop the requirement or state it as SP-12's own choice. Lands in `plans/V4-SP-12-scheduler-l3.md:2406`, `:2884`.

Wave 4 — resolve the two wave-4 merge orders.** `WP-04`, `WP-12`, `WP-13`. `plans/README.md:45` gives SP-15 → SP-16 → SP-14; `plans/V5-VERIFY-…md:7-13` gives the reverse and encodes the contradiction as a runnable `git log` check at `:24`, attributing it to `00-ARCHITECTURE.md` §9, which fixes no order and delegates.
